## [0.50.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.49.0...v0.50.0) (2026-10-08)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **auth:** email addresses are unique ignoring case. An instance where two users have the same address written with different letter case stops at the upgrade migration until each address belongs to one user (see Upgrading in the docs).

### Features

* **app:** add mcp write tools to diagnose and triage a failure ([e068f6c](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/e068f6c27d578284361b47b8e0092becb4c4e64b))

## [0.41.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.40.0...v0.41.0) (2026-09-28)

This release follows a bug from the page to the fix — Piwi Picker records it, Piwi keeps it as a failing Playwright test and follows that test until the fix holds — brings Piwi into VS Code and JetBrains IDEs, predicts the locators a change breaks before you push, and starts the Flake Lab, which ranks a flaky test's suspects from its history and replays them to make it fail on demand.

**Full diff:** [v0.40.0…v0.41.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.40.0...v0.41.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **🐞 Bug reports as failing tests** — A bug sent from Piwi Picker is kept in Piwi with its steps and evidence, written as a failing Playwright test, filed in Jira if you want, and followed until the fix holds — with the reason the suite missed it, counted as an escape.
- **🧑‍💻 Piwi in your editor** — A VS Code extension and a JetBrains plugin, built on one Piwi editor service, show CI failures, heal locators in place, open evidence and run status, apply fix plans and complete locators.
- **🔮 Preflight** — `piwi preflight` lists the test locators a change breaks before you push and applies the likely rewrites; pull requests list the locators they break and heal them from the rename.
- **🧪 Flake Lab** — A flaky test's suspects are ranked from its history and shown on the test page, the flaky list and the attempts diff; flake-lab arms replay one suspect condition next to a control.
- **🧭 Code reach** — The reporter records the source files each test executes, which widens impact analysis on unmapped files.
- **🛠️ Piwi Picker in DevTools** — A Piwi panel follows recordings and replays, ranks the selected element, and slows, fails or throttles any request — or writes a `page.route` mock from it; replays use trusted input through the debugging protocol.
- **🌐 Five languages in Piwi Picker** — The extension's interface and generated bug reports come in English and French, with German, Spanish and Brazilian Portuguese as drafts.
- **🔌 One-step connection** — Piwi Picker connects to an instance or pairs with the desktop app in one step, confirmed by a code, and reads each project's URL patterns and tests' path prefix from it.

## [0.40.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.39.0...v0.40.0) (2026-09-27)

This release reads a test's step tree — naming the hook or fixture a failure happened in and folding setup and teardown on the timeline — judges how stable each locator is and flags the brittle ones in the dashboard and in Piwi Picker, now also on Firefox, and asks Jira for exactly the fields it requires; the dashboard, server, desktop app and extension move to FSL-1.1-MIT.

**Full diff:** [v0.39.0…v0.40.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.39.0...v0.40.0)

### ⚠ Breaking changes

- The dashboard, `@piwitests/server`, the Docker image, the desktop app and the browser extension are licensed under FSL-1.1-MIT instead of MIT. The reporter and the integrations stay MIT.

### ✨ Highlights

- **🪝 Failures in hooks and fixtures** — Piwi reads the step tree, names the hook or fixture a failure happened in, and folds hooks and fixtures into Setup and Teardown on the timeline.
- **🧱 Brittle locators** — Each locator chain gets a stability judgment; brittle ones are flagged on the Locators page and in Piwi Picker's Tested elements, with a checked replacement.
- **📍 Where each locator runs** — The reporter records the page each locator call ran on, and the dashboard shows the pages each locator was used on.
- **🦊 Piwi Picker on Firefox** — The extension supports Firefox, with an AMO submission and a listing in English and French.
- **🧾 Jira that asks first** — Creating an issue or moving it through a workflow asks for the fields Jira requires, stops replaying refused creates, and shows a failure's issue where it fails.
- **🧭 Guided setup** — Jira and notification channel setup run checks and link straight to the setting to fix.
- **📚 Docs for machines** — The docs build publishes `llms.txt` and `llms-full.txt`, and generates the MCP tools, analytics widgets, metrics and reporter options pages.
- **⚖️ New license** — The dashboard, server, Docker image, desktop app and extension move to FSL-1.1-MIT; the reporter and integrations stay MIT.

## [0.39.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.38.0...v0.39.0) (2026-09-26)

This release turns the analytics page into dashboards you can save, share, show on a TV and schedule — with quality reports delivered by email, Slack, Teams, webhook or browser, in English or French — and adds per-project targets, a CI cost metric, the tested elements overlay in Piwi Picker and a Serilog sink for ASP.NET Core backend logs.

**Full diff:** [v0.38.0…v0.39.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.38.0...v0.39.0)

### ⚠ Breaking changes

- `PiwiTestLogCapture` and `PiwiTestLogEntry` now live in the `PiwiTests.Instrumentation` namespace. Code that references them directly, such as a hand-written Serilog sink, needs `using PiwiTests.Instrumentation;`.

### ✨ Highlights

- **📊 Saved dashboards** — Build dashboards from a widget catalog, save and share them, switch between them, edit them and put them on a screen in TV mode; the analytics page itself renders from the Overview dashboard.
- **📝 Quality reports** — A quality report bundles the period's numbers, previews in Export, prints from `piwi report`, and is written fully in French when you ask for it.
- **📬 Scheduled reports** — Schedule a report or a saved dashboard and receive it by email, Slack, Microsoft Teams, webhook or browser notification, with a share link, a trend chart and an optional AI narrative.
- **🎯 Targets** — Set per-project targets and see them marked on tiles, the portfolio, metric widgets, risks and the quality report.
- **💸 What flakiness costs** — Enter the cost of a CI minute and track the wasted CI cost, with new widgets for flaky debt, time to fix, suite growth, movers, ownership and environment comparison.
- **🧭 Tested elements in Piwi Picker** — The extension evaluates Playwright locator chains in the page and shows which elements a project's tests reach, per branch.
- **🔗 Numbers you can open** — Analytics numbers link to the project lists behind them, every chart exports as PNG or CSV, and the daily rollups export as JSON or CSV for BI tools and as OpenMetrics.
- **🪵 Serilog sink for ASP.NET Core** — ASP.NET Core apps can feed backend logs through a Serilog sink and choose the environments capture runs in.
- **🔔 Microsoft Teams** — Teams joins the notification channel types.

### Features

#### Dashboards
- add dashboards, quality reports and analytics enhancements ([bef22ed](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/bef22ed56f7bffebf94cdf0119d13b7a67dcc4a5))
- **db:** add the `analytics_dashboards` table and `report_schedules.dashboard_id` ([b518a19](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/b518a19366571ff5c5dae9eb86bbd9a1d323d3c9))

## [0.38.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.37.0...v0.38.0) (2026-09-24)

This release indexes every locator your tests use — a Locators tab shows which tests share each one — lets you keep a run forever, exempt from retention, and tightens who can open which settings and how OAuth sign-ins link to existing accounts.

**Full diff:** [v0.37.0…v0.38.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.37.0...v0.38.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **🔍 Locator usage index** — Piwi parses the locator chains out of stored Playwright steps and indexes which tests use each one; a Locators tab lists who uses what, and a test's page shows its own locators.
- **📌 Keep a run forever** — Mark a run to keep and retention never deletes it, from the dashboard or at ingest with the reporter's `keep` option or `PIWI_KEEP`.
- **🧭 Timeline type filter** — The evidence timeline gains a type filter.
- **🔐 Settings by role** — Settings pages enforce role-based access, and non-admin users get their own settings.
- **🔗 Safer OAuth linking** — An OAuth sign-in links only to an account whose email is verified on both sides, and changing an email clears its verified flag.

### Features

#### Locators
- add a locator usage index that tracks which tests use each locator ([11f9674](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/11f96743af97e763730aef95478b1a16c0347268))
- **app:** parse locator chains from stored Playwright steps ([c58615d](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/c58615d95cafbbba96006516a5ff73404f4bdfb6))
- **db:** index which tests use each locator chain ([ddfb5a6](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/ddfb5a651bae9ba6ac714c4aa09e66b9c8d1f876))

## [0.37.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.36.0...v0.37.0) (2026-09-23)

This release introduces the Test Map — one graph per project of what the application exposes and which tests reach it — with the scenario gaps it finds and probes that replay a passing test with one injected fault to check it would notice, and gives every test outcome and pass rate one color everywhere.

**Full diff:** [v0.36.0…v0.37.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.36.0...v0.37.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **🗺️ The Test Map** — Each project gets a graph of the routes, pages and controls the application exposes and the tests that reach them, with a feature map and an ego view over an inspector.
- **🕳️ Scenario gaps** — The Gaps tab proposes the tests the suite does not have yet — the route no test requests, the page nothing visits — each with its evidence and a next step.
- **🧪 Probes** — A probe replays a passing test with one fault injected and records whether it noticed; server probes are marked experimental.
- **🎛️ An optional capability** — The Test Map joins the capability opt-out system, so a project can decline it along with its MCP tools.
- **🎨 One color per outcome** — Every test outcome and pass rate uses one color scale across bars, charts and badges.
- **🛑 Graceful desktop stops** — Stopping a local run ends it cleanly, a failing run shows a red dot, and an interrupted run no longer leaves its progress stuck.
- **🔒 Safer fetches and SCM refs** — IPv4-mapped IPv6 addresses are blocked in outbound fetches, and SCM refs are validated and encoded to close a path traversal.

### Features

#### Test Map, scenario gaps & probes
- implement scenario gaps, probes and the feature graph ([5ea03bd](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/5ea03bd0e6fba2a4dc3c885288fdcd9ce7984ddf))
- **app:** open the Gaps tab on a feature map and make the graph an ego view over an inspector ([1b90dd9](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/1b90dd9d0d2c3a5147104ebafbd707e685514d7a))
- **app:** mark server probes experimental on Setup and the project form ([247f0c6](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/247f0c671fb5f99afc15d364cf15ddcd689d9f8e))
- **app:** list the Test Map in the product feature catalog ([4717896](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/4717896b33dcf858c240d8ce8ba75111475d8615))

#### Test Map as a capability
- **app:** register the Test Map in the capability opt-out system ([eccb030](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/eccb03028ca58fe3c0c7a1e283d75f7f3bc3a513))
- **app:** follow the Test Map capability across its surfaces ([692bc71](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/692bc71c5524a168449a70d82f176a0ef89e511b))

#### Desktop

- **app:** show dashboard links opened in the browser in the desktop window ([ff1b15b](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/ff1b15b81a838c2ff8c92e18b1d05ec37e0b5967), [831d416](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/831d416b437aa8238d9a60eda79437cb3578112c))

## [0.36.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.35.0...v0.36.0) (2026-09-22)

This release lets you choose which capabilities your instance and each project use — pick a preset on Home or decline them one by one, and the dashboard, the failure views, the AI context and the MCP tool list stop showing what you turned off — and adds locale-aware dates, self-service API keys and more ways to connect S3 and Jira.

**Full diff:** [v0.35.0…v0.36.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.35.0...v0.36.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **🎛️ Choose what you use** — Every capability can be kept or declined per instance and per project, from a presets step in the Home wizard or the Setup page; declined ones leave the navigation, the dashboard, the failure evidence and the AI context.
- **🧩 A modular MCP catalog** — Each MCP tool belongs to a module and a capability; the tool list follows your choices and the MCP page groups the catalog by module with a core-only switch.
- **🌍 Localized dates and times** — Dates and times follow a locale and time zone you pick.
- **🔑 Your own API keys** — Users manage their own API keys from the account page.
- **☁️ S3 and Jira credentials** — S3 storage accepts the AWS SDK default credential chain, and Jira accepts scoped API tokens through the Atlassian gateway.
- **🪟 Per-user Windows installer** — A per-user NSIS installer ships with its own update channel.

## [0.35.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.34.2...v0.35.0) (2026-09-19)

This release makes stored traces cheaper to keep — shared resources are reference-counted and compressed at rest, so deleting runs reclaims their space — and lets admins change a user's role without locking themselves out.

**Full diff:** [v0.34.2…v0.35.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.34.2...v0.35.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **🗜️ Smaller trace storage** — Trace event blobs and the shared resource pool are compressed at rest.
- **🧹 Deleting runs frees their traces** — Shared trace resources are reference-counted, so a partial delete reclaims the blobs no run still uses.
- **🔐 Role changes with a lockout guard** — Admins can reassign any user's role, OAuth-registered accounts included; the last administrator can never be demoted.
- **🖥️ E2E against the desktop app** — The E2E suite can run against a running desktop app.

### Features

#### Storage
- **db:** reference-count shared trace resources so partial deletes reclaim them ([ff52c77](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/ff52c7768940739b80db7ad6b4524bdcd31da716))

#### Users & roles
- **auth:** allow admins to reassign user roles with a lockout guard ([d6288b3](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/d6288b3c05a81a07fefc30be0cbd9462b02baa6f))

#### Testing
- **app:** support E2E testing against a running desktop app ([46ccc55](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/46ccc5573a2bef2bf9bb5bdef41ff372160285c8))

### Performance Improvements

- **app:** compress the trace event blob and resource pool at rest ([738d2a0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/738d2a07cf98786b3a3c2b52a52f92395c680e41), [71e591c](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/71e591c56722609f4dcf7d4e6f21e22b4770e24b))

### Bug Fixes

- **app:** free shared trace blobs when deleting runs ([681b857](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/681b857a7faed2f7abde43a33f0435292a2756af))
- **app:** populate the marker edit modal when it is opened from a chart click ([bf77c7e](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/bf77c7e7c942ae40c9fdec1b73438b89d52df150), [780c06b](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/780c06bd0f174cec901c192c50ec2c88a5f8bb96))
- **app:** show desktop run progress for watched runs, not just local ones ([110dcd2](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/110dcd2b87c087357805677ed8bf0f32efef7abf), [526c3fa](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/526c3fa5c8bf142cba8c12834757f62761722775))
- **auth:** let admins reassign the role of OAuth-registered users ([51dbd84](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/51dbd842fbd9678edc2c654aed727c0d0f876fa7))

## [0.34.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.33.0...v0.34.0) (2026-09-17)

This release brings the desktop app to Linux and puts local run progress where you already look — the taskbar, the Dock and the tray — while live run counts now add up to the planned total from the first test.

**Full diff:** [v0.33.0…v0.34.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.33.0...v0.34.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **🐧 Desktop app on Linux** — The desktop app ships `.deb`, `.rpm` and `.AppImage` builds alongside Windows and macOS.
- **📡 Run progress outside the window** — A local run shows its progress on the Windows taskbar, the macOS Dock and the tray, with status icons and a taskbar toolbar on Windows.
- **🔢 Live counts that add up** — A live run counts distinct tests and shows the planned total from the start, so its progress bar and case list agree.

### Features

#### Desktop on Linux
- **desktop:** add Linux support (`.deb`, `.rpm`, `.AppImage`) ([50bc781](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/50bc781657da2478ada0a27184a1b9c16efe6cb8))

### Bug Fixes

- **app:** count distinct tests per run and show the planned total from the start ([493efb3](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/493efb3f3efe9a6e4a744799997ccff37d172ccb), [0f7027a](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/0f7027ac034488fc4cb08b34f852072f4004ab91))

## [0.33.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.32.0...v0.33.0) (2026-09-17)

This release adds a storage analysis dashboard that tracks how your data grows, and builds out the desktop app's tooling — a local-only MCP server with file tools, an IDE launcher that opens files straight from the app, and a debug mode that forwards webview errors to the log.

**Full diff:** [v0.32.0…v0.33.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.32.0...v0.33.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **📊 Storage analysis dashboard** — A new dashboard tracks how your stored data grows over time, replacing the old storage statistics view.
- **🧱 Local-only desktop MCP** — The desktop app runs its own MCP server under a distinct name, scoped to local-only tools including file access.
- **🚀 Open files in your IDE** — Jump from the app into your editor through a command-line IDE launcher, with a test button to verify the setup.
- **🐛 Desktop debug mode** — A debug mode forwards webview runtime errors into the log, making desktop issues easier to trace.
- **🧹 Full run deletion** — Removing a run now deletes its files from disk and S3, not just its database rows.

### Features

#### Storage analysis
- **admin, ui:** a storage analysis dashboard with growth tracking, replacing the old storage statistics view, backed by a new storage analysis endpoint ([9867c57](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/9867c57e285a7e2e00c554ccb697b73f8b2ad7a3), [9f4c5b4](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/9f4c5b44962ba486efdecdda4861278eb24a8e84), [bb1ca20](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/bb1ca20c759c739d6f73904ba158738cc6b50f8d))

#### Desktop MCP & IDE launcher
- **desktop:** add local file tools to the MCP server ([92ae805](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/92ae805b184667080fcf31ccc70cc212014a0317))
- **app:** give the desktop MCP local-only tools and a distinct name ([d415622](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/d4156224b93c9e913572f74900e0e8eabfed7e9e))
- **app, desktop:** open files in your IDE via a command-line launcher, with a test button ([d357b26](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/d357b2687535f60fd5152792b3ffbdff54cee284), [13ab888](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/13ab888f3837e75a44abc15d55c0c44455c601f6))

#### Desktop diagnostics
- **app:** add a desktop debug mode and forward webview runtime errors to the log ([c33d6a1](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/c33d6a1869f4e14c81c57a0bd27fe6bbdf5f6e16), [6c51203](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/6c5120350da57782fcb36e0aaeb25c9892b897de))

### Bug Fixes

- **app:** delete run files from disk and S3 when removing a run ([f331c08](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/f331c087a8163d1e9cf42f4cdbd22bdf7eb86c4a))
- **app:** serve the DOM snapshot picker from a sandboxed endpoint, fixing a desktop CSP block ([e035f41](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/e035f41f00ca81f83ab14b3c6d8a8e32123cea7a))

## [0.32.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.31.0...v0.32.0) (2026-09-16)

This release opens evidence and run reports in their own desktop windows, renders embedded evidence from `srcdoc` iframes instead of blob URLs, and makes the reporter follow Playwright's own outcome for `test.fail()` tests.

**Full diff:** [v0.31.0…v0.32.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.31.0...v0.32.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **🪟 Multi-window desktop evidence** — Open captured evidence and full run reports in their own windows in the desktop app, with evidence windows opening maximized.
- **🖼️ More reliable evidence rendering** — Embedded iframes, including failure-time DOM snapshots, now render from `srcdoc` rather than blob URLs.
- **✅ Truer `test.fail()` outcomes** — The reporter now follows Playwright's own result for `test.fail()` tests.

## [0.29.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.28.0...v0.29.0) (2026-09-13)

This release connects Piwi to your issue tracker: file a Jira ticket straight from a failure or cluster, keep it in two-way sync in the language your team uses, and track what still needs one — plus richer PDF exports and one-click import of a linked folder's past runs.

**Full diff:** [v0.28.0…v0.29.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.28.0...v0.29.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **🎫 Jira integration** — File an issue from a failure or cluster and keep it linked, with an inbox queue for everything that still needs a ticket.
- **🔁 Two-way status sync** — A background sync task and an inbound Jira webhook keep ticket state and Piwi in step, and you can trigger a sweep on demand.
- **🌐 Tickets in your language** — Issues are written in the destination's language (en/fr), backed by a per-project AI response-language setting.
- **📄 Richer PDF exports** — Exported executions and clusters now carry syntax-highlighted source and frame screenshots.
- **🗂️ Import past runs** — Linking a local project folder in the desktop app offers to import its previous runs.

### Features

#### Issue tracker & Jira
- **File Jira issues from failures and keep them in sync** ([51f1bb0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/51f1bb02534da69f6d869524e92466613d42156b))
- Integration provider layer with connection-aware links ([e7b06d0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/e7b06d03754d44baffba609decf8206daad577e9)), and a connections API with Jira env vars ([23da674](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/23da6747f2fdd1479098bf3429f64f80e4eff4e7))
- Create-issue outbox, flow and API ([fde45f4](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/fde45f4941f9143a9bd1f48d8d0553dad42b42fa)), rendering issue bodies and building cluster/execution tickets ([0b32413](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/0b324135d41a076bd27e45ab2557dceb235e31f1))
- Needs-ticket inbox queue and ticket-done reconcile ([cf36995](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/cf36995c44a4219676b5b144017ffcaaa7428aef))
- Project binding endpoints and two-way sync policies ([81d796f](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/81d796f8f3273f21be08a96d25f17c6f2717fb7e)), with a resolved binding shape ([cf75768](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/cf75768ade3eddf691b291ebbfbb2b1a43e578a1))
- Tracker status sync task and inbound Jira webhook ([e1977cd](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/e1977cdc13274f0b61213556c4a12f89a4dc5c98)), plus an on-demand sync sweep from an admin endpoint ([913e85d](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/913e85d8ba152c4c69bec295ee08ed7c6f09f2f5))
- Carry the known issue key across the workflow ([f2c7596](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/f2c75964fe69c0460ff14525c83e07f5e42bb752))
- Settings integrations page and docs ([b8d54eb](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/b8d54eb7f644d87c787308b39f571c7aee26b827))
- **ui:** create-issue modal and entry points ([d30270e](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/d30270e7b19f46f1a7203c890b1d8e4bb5ccd1be)), and a project binding form with a connection activity list ([a40a08b](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/a40a08b5c0fdc2547786d27668dc748e2dfa942d))
- **db:** integration connections, actions and entity-link columns ([72fb33f](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/72fb33fbdf0942b3a8bfab2297eae0602acb7f5b))
- **demo:** mirror the create-issue flow with a canned Jira connection ([bc27d7b](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/bc27d7b2744b7fb1d3df490a8ad10355ecff0082)) and the project binding and webhook-token endpoints ([740ce0f](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/740ce0f6934eb4e809a1dc5b8dc359aa99a3e23b))

#### Tickets in your language

## [0.28.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.27.0...v0.28.0) (2026-09-12)

This release makes the git branch and environment a first-class part of every run, puts the failing step's screenshot and ARIA tree right on the timeline, and exports executions as generated PDFs — while tightening memory use on large runs.

**Full diff:** [v0.27.0…v0.28.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.27.0...v0.28.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **🌿 Branch- and environment-aware baselines** — Pick the base branch a run compares against; the reporter captures a pull request's target branch automatically, and branch/environment names show with their icon across the app.
- **🖼️ Failing step on the timeline** — The failing step's screenshot and ARIA tree now appear inline in trace snapshots and on the timeline.
- **📄 PDF export without the browser** — Export executions and clusters as generated PDFs, with no browser print dialog.
- **🩹 Self-healing desktop server** — The bundled server auto-restarts if it crashes.
- **🧠 Bounded memory on large runs** — Trace ingestion and the reporter's stream buffer are capped, and case-file uploads stream to disk, to stop out-of-memory on big runs.

### Features

#### Branch & environment
- **app:** environment-aware run baseline with a selectable base branch ([3d238a0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/3d238a0efcf9bf33bf897d08e44b76b06f891542), [aa96a1c](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/aa96a1c14d473fd0346cb0d9524625b4c8023d76))
- **reporter:** capture the pull request's target branch as the base branch ([306f382](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/306f382d087a359aacb4cf71ce06efb0a0186b00))
- **ui:** show branch and environment names with their icon everywhere ([8ce8946](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/8ce89465d98235d53560d9ef5ef6510d4cfb4674))

#### Failing-step evidence
- **app:** surface the failing step's aria tree in trace snapshots ([179fb04](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/179fb047b706b531e0e9f91874576c301e64de84))
- **ui:** attach the failing step's screenshot and ARIA to the timeline ([d0a49ea](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/d0a49eaa6d803548dffcb916c5e0e533288c94c0), [293fed2](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/293fed26de406bd3a2f75dea2398f1359e894e68))

#### PDF export
- **app:** export executions and clusters as generated PDFs without browser print ([ae480f1](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/ae480f1a05685ef50b80a29f5b1e1a17a9a9f92d), [21263e8](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/21263e8b2240467706a86b953c786776c42139c0))

#### Resilience
- **app:** auto-restart the bundled desktop server if it crashes ([a141645](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/a141645d855ac09d428e0c6e89452dc32d2094ea), [e6cdfc4](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/e6cdfc4c573e96858f3dc2fb1b488d5ddb8a7118))
- **reporter:** bound the in-memory stream buffer ([e318a6e](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/e318a6ebcc2aeac7c1558b1d4c729ada9c2f882d), [32e325c](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/32e325cc2dc16a4cc9a176d558b39bb7b4efb4fd))

### Bug Fixes

- **ai:** stop forcing `temperature=0` on OpenAI-compatible calls ([fca04f1](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/fca04f1ee2d0fb7fc3ebcfd656849c5d36dbb7ab), [78f771c](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/78f771c69ea595aafed93a2656a59c06027b7670))
- **app:** bound trace-ingestion memory to stop OOM on large runs and the desktop server ([f5376d8](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/f5376d8a2e85ec66239e1e67dfcf676d7df2cb49), [6523433](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/652343308c9c96a7b2b0de53f00b971fe6eb75d6))
- **app:** stream case-files uploads to disk to bound ingestion memory ([8a75f43](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/8a75f43a4722d847adbf023071dbda556f72666f))
- **auth:** map `PIWI_OAUTH_*` onto the prebuilt server's runtime config ([ac72f06](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/ac72f06a5a7ab112cacfd40c60c0389d86e8a76c), [29edb7c](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/29edb7c5e73168cc1d608a2f653820dbc57d6ec1))
- **auth:** read Google's `verified_email` flag when mapping the userinfo payload ([702d0f6](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/702d0f67f3928ed3f5106ff0772a7abc71c2de8e), [d5f8f0f](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/d5f8f0f94e4671c95d03ee02d063eaf2e04b0d6e))
- **demo:** stop the demo serving a stale SPA shell after redeploys, with cache-busting on shell and seed fetches ([00dc542](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/00dc542fe9259ab716038ee6701641f4fcb85cbd), [428a29a](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/428a29abe0cfb825c400304a2048dcb2f6aa7046))
- **reporter:** fail a request when the connection closes before the body completes ([b104d12](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/b104d120245c0f8000b6b99d1dd472674482660f), [c7b9911](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/c7b9911c8c360ca2d9fd34ef6d8d2ca29c4fb151))

- **ui:** align branch and environment labels inline and copy the branch on click ([54a3ab9](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/54a3ab97bab8c7bb17139482713b1cab9357e61a))

### Performance

## [0.27.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.26.1...v0.27.0) (2026-09-07)

The biggest release yet: Piwi now explains why a test failed and what to do about it — deterministic clues with a one-line headline, a cross-project failure inbox with bulk triage, one-click reproduce and git-bisect, flaky attempt diffs, page-structure diffs against the last green run, and fix verification that closes the loop — on rebuilt failure pages and with full Playwright 1.63 support.

**Full diff:** [v0.26.1…v0.27.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.26.1...v0.27.0)

### ✨ Highlights

- **🧩 Deterministic failure clues** — A rules engine explains every failure in one line before the raw error, no AI required.
- **📥 Failure inbox & bulk triage** — Open clusters across projects land in a Home inbox you can filter, snooze, assign, quarantine and triage in bulk — also through MCP.
- **🔁 Reproduce & git-bisect** — Generate a local repro recipe and a git bisect for a failure, run them from the desktop app, and persist the first bad commit on the cluster.
- **🎞️ Flaky attempt diff** — An Attempts tab diffs a flaky test's passing and failing attempts and feeds the flaky classifier.
- **🧬 Page-structure diff** — Sample green ARIA snapshots and diff the page structure against the last green run.
- **✅ Fix verification** — A partial run can verify a fix when it covers the whole cluster; the fixer is notified and can re-run the affected tests in CI.
- **🧭 Rebuilt failure pages** — Execution, cluster, run, project and history pages rebuilt into one column with a situation block, tabbed evidence and shared building blocks.
- **🎭 Playwright 1.63** — Step subtitles and params, test locks, browser dialogs, `.visible()` narrowing and contrast, captured end to end.
- **🔬 Evidence straight from the trace** — Console, network and ARIA are derived from the trace without capture fixtures, on one timeline.
- **📤 Perfetto export** — Export runs and executions as Perfetto traces.

### Features

#### Deterministic clues & one-line failures
- **app:** add the deterministic failure-clue engine ([d78ebfa](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/d78ebfa08b89b18ca102897c43fafc81bd1fa3a7)), load and serve clues per execution ([902d5b3](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/902d5b3293a261e64c402c90557c4e41d3dfb9e5)), and surface a clue for every failure ([72a10a3](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/72a10a3cb662c6dc82466a093d3235effc47116e))
- **ui:** surface deterministic clues on the diagnosis tab ([d80c4c2](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/d80c4c217916344e65393f8a2f27afc9aa475d88))
- **ai:** feed clues to diagnosis and spend the auto-diagnose budget on the unclued ([b402cd1](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/b402cd1693131b41f162ae4bcade6541a228ed56)); base diagnosis staleness on the current evidence ([2d73843](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/2d73843c5bcef160be007d701486e186d6996376))
- **app:** parse Playwright errors into a structured record and a one-line headline ([3dfb5dd](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/3dfb5dd9fc999483692d75db27a6366baa19eeac)), and explain a failure in one line before the raw error ([8d1ca43](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/8d1ca432ce68415db50a53c772c055d5b1baaf49), [a7a6a60](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/a7a6a60f9782f469bf32d8e9306785657ac4947d))
- **app:** compute the story, situation, cluster state and next step for failure pages ([4293596](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/4293596ef94fad2d0e4d96db9f964daab4287312), [530e18e](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/530e18ef6514498f99804eedc152a778081e42a2))

## [0.26.1](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.26.0...v0.26.1) (2026-08-29)

A stability and accessibility pass over the run, execution and failure pages: correct sharded-run accounting, hydration-safe dates, clearer navigation, and a sweep of accessibility semantics.

**Full diff:** [v0.26.0…v0.26.1](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.26.0...v0.26.1)

### ✨ Highlights

- **♿ Accessibility pass** — Landmarks, named icon buttons, progressbar semantics and announced status-bar segments across the run and failure pages.
- **🧮 Correct sharded-run accounting** — Executions are counted once (not per event and finish) and run status is judged by final attempts, not failed-attempt counters.
- **💧 Hydration-safe dates & tooltips** — Absolute dates render client-only and history tooltips are deterministic, so server and client agree.
- **🧭 Clearer run navigation** — Tabs renamed to Executions and Failure clusters, disabled tabs explained, empty states swept, and tab help wired in.

### Bug Fixes

#### Accessibility & semantics
- **ui:** announce every status-bar segment ([0927c97](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/0927c976ce185373d08cf9a1b9a04445b2ecfeee))
- **ui:** main landmark, named icon buttons, triage badge and a gated AI disclaimer ([7110791](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/71107914299e6fdba1912a6b5791ba698e3b492d))
- **ui:** progressbar semantics, interrupted-run hints and a mobile scroll model ([89990fe](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/89990fe5cf60c9098c5f32cb1002e503078e6d9e))
- **ui:** use navigation semantics for page tab strips ([fedba50](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/fedba50f617a973a369ec89555581c4f1a1239ec))
- **ui:** reset the list-view override and gate mobile table roles ([785fc4b](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/785fc4b3e7e158bfb138cd1f47897833128b1e4a))

#### Run, execution & cluster pages
- **app:** resolve review findings on the test-run page UX ([408f1f8](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/408f1f854c2dc9085ad48fa6cff414fbccc0b542)), and further test-run page UX fixes ([98c5baf](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/98c5baf128609b2fda1ee632aa96474c7b4709b7))
- **ui:** branch execution tabs on status and reserve diagnosis during live runs ([1edcf1a](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/commit/1edcf1a3a16b76f94778409964f7cb3ea5ecf9f5))

## [0.8.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.7.0...v0.8.0) (2026-07-09)

Version bump from adopting release-please ([#215](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/issues/215)); the tag was never published — its changes shipped with 0.9.0.

## [0.7.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.6.0...v0.7.0) (2026-07-08)

### Features

* AI diagnosis fetches full source files and validates suggested patches ([#202](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/202))
* redesigned failure-cluster detail page layout ([#205](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/205))
* failure tab UX: URL persistence and smart defaults ([#207](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/207))
* data-grounded demo AI diagnosis + hardened diagnosis engine ([#208](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/208))
* reusable responsive primitives for mobile screens ([#209](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/209))

### Bug Fixes

* locator-healing pipeline issues from reporter capture to diagnosis ([#201](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/201))
* **ai:** provider correctness + token-efficiency for model calls ([#206](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/206))
* demo service-worker crash in `deleteProject` ([#210](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/210))

### Maintenance

* AI context-builder audit ([#200](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/200)), Vite warmup ([#203](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/203)), docs feature screenshots ([#204](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/204)), CI storage × database test matrix ([#211](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/pull/211))

## [0.6.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.5.0...v0.6.0) (2026-07-02)

### Features

* **MCP server expanded from 14 to 38 tools** — global search, run insights, spec health, slow tests, performance & stability trends, network requests, failure groups, repo commits & diffs, test catalog, traces, tags, links, recent activity, instance stats
* AI failure-cluster workflow via MCP — list open clusters, diagnose, explain failures, set status & base commit, submit feedback
* locator healing exposed through MCP with enriched healing context
* timeline span-type filter — data-driven toggles for tests, setup, hooks, fixtures & wasted waits
* OpenAPI spec generated at build time and served at `/_openapi.json` in production

### Improvements

* full reporter refactor with a modular internal architecture and new architecture docs
* improved AI context building: data aggregation, error fingerprinting, source snippets, trace parsing
* MCP hardening from an external API audit: pagination cursors, enforced project scope, richer flaky stats
* timeline fixes (wasted-time bars persisting), more data on test-case pages, more realistic demo seed

## [0.5.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.4.4...v0.5.0) (2026-06-28)

### Features

* locator healing: capture & suggestion of alternative locators, signature-based matching, convention-preserving fix recommendation
* backend logs shown under each network request in the test-case view
* home page revamp with filtering by environment and run type; settings revamp with env-var info
* test screenshots sent to AI diagnosis and embedded in HTML export
* user display names and optional passwords; demo "act as" user switcher
* **reporter:** heartbeat for fast interrupted-run detection

### Improvements & fixes

* diagnosis streaming and evidence-quality improvements; improved failure-cluster page
* run insights UX with baseline detection and empty states
* Docker image optimization and Docker Hub repository update
* fixed 404 redirect logic and test-case grid counts

## [0.4.0 – 0.4.4](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.3.0...v0.4.4) (2026-06-23 – 2026-06-28)

Stabilization series following 0.3.0 — publish-pipeline, demo, and test-suite fixes ([release notes](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/releases)).

## [0.3.0](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.2.1...v0.3.0) (2026-06-23)

### Features

* diagnosis page with enhanced AI diagnosis (multiple hypotheses & severity levels)
* global search across all projects; new-regression tracking
* sharding support with improved shard timeline
* backend logs with test-tree visualization; custom run names
* notifications & subscriptions for projects + account management
* MCP server integration; home wizard
* contextual help blocks throughout the UI

### Internal

* component reorganization, AI context-building refactor, test suite migrated to Vitest

## [0.1.0 – 0.2.1](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/compare/v0.0.8...v0.2.1) (2026-04-26 – 2026-06-22)

* multi-report support; project tagging with search and tag filtering
* storage stats page, test-run deletion, and bulk cleanup
* breadcrumb navigation and improved page titles
* VitePress documentation site with GitHub Pages deployment
* S3 storage tests with MinIO in CI; reporter authentication support
* reporter first published to npm
* project renamed to **Piwi Dashboard** (from "Playwright Dashboard") to avoid confusion with Microsoft's Playwright

## [0.0.1 – 0.0.8](https://github.com/PiwiTests/platform/releases) (2025-12 – 2026-04)

Early development: initial dashboard and Playwright reporter, run/case detail pages, metadata collection, performance tracking with network-request analysis and web vitals, native zlib compression, multistage Docker build.
